                    <script>
                        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
                            var navbarHeader = document.getElementById("navbar-header");
                            var menu = document.getElementById("brandkit-menu");

                            navbarHeader.addEventListener("contextmenu", rightClick);
                            document.addEventListener("click", hideMenu);

                            function toggleMenu(e) {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                if (menu.style.display === "block") {
                                    hideMenu();
                                } else {
                                    menu.style.display = "block";
                                }
                            }

                            function hideMenu() {
                                menu.style.display = "none";
                            }

                            function rightClick(e) {
                                e.preventDefault();
                                e.stopPropagation();

                                if (menu.style.display === "block") {
                                    hideMenu();
                                } else {
                                    menu.style.display = "block";
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    </script>

    <script>
    function toggleNavigation() {
        let mobileNavDropdown = $("#qc-navbar-collapse-mobile");
        mobileNavDropdown.toggleClass('hidden');
        if (mobileNavDropdown.hasClass('hidden')) {
            window.removeEventListener('touchmove', blockNavigationTouchPropagtation, {passive: false});
        } else {
            window.addEventListener('touchmove', blockNavigationTouchPropagtation, {passive: false});
        }
    }

    function blockNavigationTouchPropagtation(event) {
        if (!$("#qc-navbar-collapse-mobile").hasClass('hidden')) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        }
    }
</script>

            <h1 class="automated">Trading and Orders</h1><div id="05-Position-Sizing" class="floating-anchor-h2"></div><h2 class="page-sub-heading">
                            <span>Position Sizing</span>
                            <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03 Writing Algorithms/22 Trading and Orders/05 Position Sizing" class="anchor-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true" style="font-size:0.7em;"></i></a>
                            <a href="#05-Position-Sizing" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                        </h2><div class="sub-headings-area"><div class="row section ">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="01-Introduction" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Introduction
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/01%20Introduction.html" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#01-Introduction" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>LEAN provides several methods to help you set specific portfolio weights for assets. These methods account for lot size and pre-calculated order fees.</p></div>  <textarea class="sub-heading-content-editor" contenteditable="true" data-sub-heading-path="/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/01%20Introduction.html"></textarea>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div><div class="row section not-template">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="02-Single-Asset-Targets" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Single Asset Targets
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/02%20Single%20Asset%20Targets.html" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#02-Single-Asset-Targets" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>The <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method calculates the number of asset units to purchase based on the portfolio weight you provide and then <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/trading-and-orders/order-types/market-orders#02-Place-Orders'>submits market orders</a>. This method provides a quick way to set up a portfolio with a set of weights for assets. If you already have holdings, you may want to liquidate the existing holdings first to free up buying power.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">// Allocate 50% of portfolio value to IBM
SetHoldings("IBM", 0.5);

// Allocate 50% of portfolio value to IBM, but liquidate other holdings first
SetHoldings("IBM", 0.5, liquidateExistingHoldings: true);

// Provide a tag and order properties to the SetHoldings method
SetHoldings(symbol, weight, liquidateExistingHoldings, tag, orderProperties);
</pre> <pre class="python"> # Allocate 50% of buying power to IBM
self.set_holdings("IBM", 0.5)

# Allocate 50% of portfolio value to IBM, but liquidate other holdings first
self.set_holdings("IBM", 0.5, liquidate_existing_holdings=True)

# Provide a tag and order properties to the SetHoldings method
self.set_holdings(symbol, weight, liquidate_existing_holdings, tag, order_properties)</pre>
</div> <p>  If the percentage you provide translates to an order quantity of 0 or to a small quantity that doesn't exceed the <code class='python'>minimum_order_margin_portfolio_percentage</code><code class='csharp'>MinimumOrderMarginPortfolioPercentage</code> <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/initialization#10-Set-Algorithm-Settings'>algorithm setting</a>, then the <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method doesn’t place an order and doesn’t log anything.
</p>
</div>  <textarea class="sub-heading-content-editor" contenteditable="true" data-sub-heading-path="/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/02%20Single%20Asset%20Targets.html"></textarea>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div><div class="row section not-template">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="03-Multiple-Asset-Targets" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Multiple Asset Targets
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/03%20Multiple%20Asset%20Targets.html" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#03-Multiple-Asset-Targets" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>When you trade a weighted basket of assets, sometimes you must intelligently scale down existing positions before increasing allocations to other assets. If you call the <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method with a list of <code>PortfolioTarget</code> objects, LEAN sorts the orders based on your position delta and then <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/trading-and-orders/order-types/market-orders#02-Place-Orders'>places the orders</a> that reduce your position size in an asset before it places orders that increase your position size in an asset. When you call the <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method with a list of <code>PortfolioTarget</code> objects, the decimal you pass to the <code>PortfolioTarget</code> constructor represents the portfolio weight. In this situation, don't use the <code class="csharp">PortfolioTarget.Percent</code><code class="python">PortfolioTarget.percent</code> method.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">// Purchase a portfolio of targets, processing orders intelligently.
// PortfolioTarget accepts a Symbol and decimal percent of buying power.
var targets = new List&lt;PortfolioTarget&gt;() { new ("SPY", 0.8m), new ("IBM", 0.2m) };
SetHoldings(targets);
</pre> <pre class="python"># Purchase a portfolio of targets, processing orders intelligently.
# PortfolioTarget accepts a Symbol and decimal percent of buying power.
targets = [PortfolioTarget("SPY", 0.8), PortfolioTarget("IBM", 0.2)]
self.set_holdings(targets)</pre>
</div> <p>If you want the targets you pass to define the target portfolio state, enable the <code class="csharp">liquidateExistingHoldings</code><code class="python">liquidate_existing_holdings</code> argument.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">SetHoldings(targets, liquidateExistingHoldings: true);</pre> <pre class="python">self.set_holdings([PortfolioTarget("SPY", 0.8), PortfolioTarget("IBM", 0.2)], liquidate_existing_holdings=True)</pre>
</div> <p>The method also accepts <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/trading-and-orders/order-properties#17-Tags'>tag</a> and <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/trading-and-orders/order-properties'>order properties</a> arguments.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">SetHoldings(targets, liquidateExistingHoldings, tag, orderProperties);</pre> <pre class="python">self.set_holdings(targets, liquidate_existing_holdings, tag, order_properties)</pre>
</div> <p>  If the percentage you provide translates to an order quantity of 0 or to a small quantity that doesn't exceed the <code class='python'>minimum_order_margin_portfolio_percentage</code><code class='csharp'>MinimumOrderMarginPortfolioPercentage</code> <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/initialization#10-Set-Algorithm-Settings'>algorithm setting</a>, then the <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method doesn’t place an order and doesn’t log anything.
</p>
</div>  <textarea class="sub-heading-content-editor" contenteditable="true" data-sub-heading-path="/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/03%20Multiple%20Asset%20Targets.html"></textarea>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div><div class="row section not-template">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="04-Asynchronous-Orders" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Asynchronous Orders
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/04%20Asynchronous%20Orders.php" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#04-Asynchronous-Orders" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>When you trade a large portfolio of assets, you may want to send orders in batches and not wait for the response of each one. To send <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/trading-and-orders/key-concepts#09-Asynchronous-Execution'>asynchronous orders</a>, set the <code>asynchronous</code> argument to <code class="csharp">true</code><code class="python">True</code>.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">SetHoldings("IBM", 0.5, asynchronous: true);</pre> <pre class="python">self.set_holdings("IBM", 0.5, asynchronous=True);</pre>
</div> <p>When you set multiple asset targets, you likely want to send asynchronous orders.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">var targets = new List&lt;PortfolioTarget&gt;() { new("SPY", 0.8m), new("IBM", 0.2m) };
SetHoldings(targets, asynchronous: true);
</pre> <pre class="python">targets = [PortfolioTarget("SPY", 0.8), PortfolioTarget("IBM", 0.2)]
self.set_holdings(targets, asynchronous=True)</pre>
</div></div>  <textarea class="sub-heading-content-editor" contenteditable="true" data-sub-heading-path="/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/04%20Asynchronous%20Orders.php"></textarea>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div><div class="row section not-template">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="05-Calculate-Order-Quantities" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Calculate Order Quantities
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/05%20Calculate%20Order%20Quantities.html" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#05-Calculate-Order-Quantities" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>The <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method uses market order to reach the target portfolio weight you provide. If you want to use a different order type, you need to specify the quantity to trade. To calculate the number of units to trade based on a portfolio weight, call the <code class="csharp">CalculateOrderQuantity</code><code class="python">calculate_order_quantity</code> method. The method calculates the quantity based on the current price of the asset and adjusts it for the <a href='/docs/v2/writing-algorithms/reality-modeling/transaction-fees/key-concepts'>fee model</a> of the security. The target weight you provide is an unleveraged value. For instance, if you have 2x leverage and request a 100% weight, the method calculates the quantity that uses half of your available margin.</p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">// Calculate the fee-adjusted quantity of shares with given buying power
var quantity = CalculateOrderQuantity("IBM", 0.4);
</pre> <pre class="python"> # Calculate the fee-adjusted quantity of shares with given buying power
quantity = self.calculate_order_quantity("IBM", 0.4)
</pre>
</div>
</div>  <textarea class="sub-heading-content-editor" contenteditable="true" data-sub-heading-path="/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/05%20Calculate%20Order%20Quantities.html"></textarea>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div><div class="row section not-template">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="06-Buying-Power-Buffer" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Buying Power Buffer
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/06%20Buying%20Power%20Buffer.html" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#06-Buying-Power-Buffer" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>If you place a market on open order near the market close, the market can gap overnight to a worse open price. If the gap is large enough against your trade direction, you may not have sufficient buying power at the open to fill your trade. To ensure a high probability of order fills through market gaps and discontinuities, the <code class="csharp">SetHoldings</code><code class="python">set_holdings</code> method assumes a 0.25% cash buffer. If LEAN rejects your orders due to buying power, widen the cash buffer through the algorithm <code class="csharp">Settings</code><code class="python">settings</code> property.<br></p>

<div class="section-example-container"> <pre class="csharp">// Set the cash buffer to 5%
Settings.FreePortfolioValuePercentage = 0.05m;

// Set the cash buffer to $10,000
Settings.FreePortfolioValue = 10000m;</pre> <pre class="python"># Set the cash buffer to 5%
self.settings.free_portfolio_value_percentage = 0.05

# Set the cash buffer to $10,000
self.settings.free_portfolio_value = 10000</pre>
</div> <p>If you use <code class="csharp">FreePortfolioValuePercentage</code><code class="python">free_portfolio_value_percentage</code>, you must set it in the <code class="csharp">Initialize</code><code class="python">initialize</code> or <code class="csharp">PostInitialize</code><code class="python">post_initialize</code> event handler. If you use <code class="csharp">FreePortfolioValue</code><code class="python">free_portfolio_value</code>, you must set it after the <code class="csharp">PostInitialize</code><code class="python">post_initialize</code> event handler.</p>
</div>  <textarea class="sub-heading-content-editor" contenteditable="true" data-sub-heading-path="/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/06%20Buying%20Power%20Buffer.html"></textarea>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div><div class="row section not-template">
                            <div class="col-xs-12 section-container">
                                <div class="section-copy">
                                    <div id="99-Examples" class="floating-anchor-h3"></div>
                                    <h3 class="" style="">
                                        Examples
                                        <a href="https://github.com/QuantConnect/Documentation/tree/master/03%20Writing%20Algorithms/22%20Trading%20and%20Orders/05%20Position%20Sizing/99%20Examples.html" class="anchor-link source-file-link" target="_blank"><i class="fa fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a href="#99-Examples" class="anchor-link"><i class="fa fa-anchor" aria-hidden="true"></i></a>
                                        <a class="anchor-link clickable edit-sub-heading not-editor" onclick="Documentation.EditSubHeading(this)"><i class="fa fa-pencil"></i></a>
                            <a class="anchor-link clickable save-sub-heading" onclick="Documentation.SaveSubHeading(this)" style="display: none"><i class="fa fa-floppy-o"></i></a>
                                    </h3>
                                    <div class="sub-heading-content"><p>The following examples demonstrate some common practices for position sizing.</p>

<h4>Example 1: Covered Call</h4> <p></p>
<div class="section-example-container testable"> <pre class="csharp">public class PositionSizingAlgorithm : QCAlgorithm
{
    private Symbol _spy;

    public override void Initialize()
    {
        SetStartDate(2024, 9, 1);
        SetEndDate(2024, 12, 31);
        SetCash(10_000_000);
        
        // Seed the price to ensure securities have information for margin calculations.
        Settings.SeedInitialPrices = true;
        
        // Request SPY data for trading. We use raw price data to compare the strike price fairly for ATM contract selection.
        _spy = AddEquity("SPY", dataNormalizationMode: DataNormalizationMode.Raw).Symbol;
        
        // Set a scheduled event to rebalance the covered call positions.
        Schedule.On(
            DateRules.WeekStart(_spy),
            TimeRules.AfterMarketOpen(_spy, 1),
            Rebalance
        );
    }

    private void Rebalance()
    {
        var optionChain = OptionChain(_spy);
        // Filter for the calls within 1-week expiry.
        var expiry = optionChain.Where(x =&gt; x.Expiry &lt;= Time.AddDays(6))
            .Max(x =&gt; x.Expiry);
        // Select the ATM call.
        var atmCall = optionChain.Where(x =&gt; x.Right == OptionRight.Call &amp;&amp; x.Expiry == expiry)
            .OrderBy(x =&gt; Math.Abs(x.Strike - Securities[_spy].Price))
            .First();
        var atmCallSymbol = AddOptionContract(atmCall).Symbol;

        // Calculate the order size for the covered call.
        var quantity = (int)Math.Floor(CalculateOrderQuantity(_spy, 0.5) / 100m);
        
        // Covered call involves 100 SPY shares and a -1 ATM call contract.
        if (quantity &gt; 0)
        {
            ComboMarketOrder(
            [
                Leg.Create(_spy, 100),
                Leg.Create(atmCallSymbol, -1)
            ], quantity,
            asynchronous: true);
        }
    }
}</pre> <pre class="python">class PositionSizingAlgorithm(QCAlgorithm):
    def initialize(self) -&gt; None:
        self.set_start_date(2024, 9, 1)
        self.set_end_date(2024, 12, 31)
        self.set_cash(10_000_000)
        
        # Seed the price to ensure securities have information for margin calculations.
        self.settings.seed_initial_prices = True
        
        # Request SPY data for trading. We use raw price data to compare the strike price fairly for ATM contract selection.
        self.spy = self.add_equity("SPY", data_normalization_mode=DataNormalizationMode.RAW).symbol
        
        # Set a scheduled event to rebalance the covered call positions.
        self.schedule.on(
            self.date_rules.week_start(self.spy),
            self.time_rules.after_market_open(self.spy, 1),
            self.rebalance
        )

    def rebalance(self) -&gt; None:
        option_chain = self.option_chain(self.spy)
        # Filter for the calls within 1-week expiry.
        expiry = max(x.expiry for x in option_chain if x.expiry &lt;= self.time + timedelta(6))
        filtered = [x for x in option_chain if x.right == OptionRight.CALL and x.expiry == expiry]
        # Select the ATM call.
        atm_call = sorted(filtered, key=lambda x: abs(x.strike - self.securities[self.spy].price))[0]
        atm_call = self.add_option_contract(atm_call).symbol

        # Calculate the order size for the covered call.
        quantity = int(self.calculate_order_quantity(self.spy, 0.5) // 100)
        # Covered call involves 100 shares of SPY and -1 ATM call contract.
        if quantity &gt; 0:
            self.combo_market_order(
                [Leg.create(self.spy, 100), Leg.create(atm_call, -1)], 
                quantity,
                asynchronous=True
            )</pre>
</div>
